# Six contacts: a lower point and a noncentral top quadrilateral

**Theorem.** Assume the BTTT normalization of N6_TOP_TRIPLE_SETUP.md:
P lies inside HABC, while Q,R,S lie in the top cap with EQR,EQS,ERS>0.
Suppose their four-sign word (FQR,FQS,FRS,QRS) is -++- or ++-+.
Then some actual triangle has unsigned doubled area less than 93/1000.
Equivalently, the two single-ear top-quadrilateral alternatives are excluded.
This theorem covers these entire top-hull alternatives; it does not assert
closure of the other BTTT or ITTT alternatives.

Every continuous hypothesis, initial coordinate box, exact interval step,
geometric rule and nonnegative factor is specified in N6_TOP_TRIPLE_SETUP.md.
The proof below is a finite exhaustive case tree with literal rational data.
No discovery result is an additional premise. All actual determinants have
nonzero sign. A contact-order cut ORDER-/ORDER+ means ac<=bh or ac>=bh;
these two weak cases overlap on equality.

## 1. Exhaustive leaves

The first two fields after each row name give the location word and root
top-sign word. The last field is its complete additional path. P denotes a
fixed polynomial contradiction, G a geometric contradiction, and B an
empty exact interval union. At every internal prefix, the two next tokens
are opposite signs of the same actual determinant or the same weak
contact-order comparison. Thus the printed leaves cover each listed root.

```n6-top-roots
BTTT -++-
BTTT ++-+
```

```n6-top-leaves
R001 BTTT -++- P APQ-,APR-,HBP-
R002 BTTT -++- P APQ-,APR-,HBP+,ORDER-
R003 BTTT -++- P APQ-,APR-,HBP+,ORDER+
R004 BTTT -++- P APQ-,APR+
R005 BTTT -++- P APQ+,BPQ-,ACP-
R006 BTTT -++- P APQ+,BPQ-,ACP+,HBP-
R007 BTTT -++- P APQ+,BPQ-,ACP+,HBP+
R008 BTTT -++- P APQ+,BPQ+,ACP-
R009 BTTT -++- P APQ+,BPQ+,ACP+,ORDER-
R010 BTTT -++- P APQ+,BPQ+,ACP+,ORDER+
R011 BTTT ++-+ P APQ-,APR-,HBP-
R012 BTTT ++-+ P APQ-,APR-,HBP+,ORDER-

R014 BTTT ++-+ P APQ-,APR+,HBP-
R015 BTTT ++-+ P APQ-,APR+,HBP+,ORDER-

R020 BTTT ++-+ P APQ+,BPQ+,ACP-
R021 BTTT ++-+ P APQ+,BPQ+,ACP+,ORDER-
R022 BTTT ++-+ P APQ+,BPQ+,ACP+,ORDER+
```

## 2. Reading the fixed data

Every trace line is one of the setup note's exact geometric rules.
Each polynomial leaf prints its box version, weakened endpoint costs,
whether its central lower-coordinate refinement is used, and both its
initial and final boxes in variable order a,b,c,e,f,h,p,q,u,v,r,w,j,k.
The initial box is the displayed formula for that version; a version-3
leaf lists every rational interval refinement. An interval record prints
the factor, target coordinate index, old/raw/new intervals, and all distinct
endpoint slope/intercept pairs. Reconstructing those finite lines verifies
the step by MULTIAFFINE_BOX_BOUNDS.md. Indices start at zero.

A witness row names the actual interior points for its factor; a dash
denotes the empty set. A rate row gives the rational support coefficient.
A square row gives the fifteen integer coefficients of an affine form in
1 and the fourteen coordinates shifted by the final lower endpoints.
The sum rows are positive integer weights followed by products of named
nonnegative factors. The bound is the exact coefficient upper estimate
from the setup note and is strictly negative.

## 3. Assembly

Every listed path has been contradicted by the preceding fixed data.
At each internal prefix the two possible next signs are both covered,
including both weak contact orders where applicable. Therefore neither
of the theorem's two top-hull alternatives can have all actual areas
at least s. This proves the stated complete exclusion.

```n6-top-totals

```

The optional standard-library checker reconstructs this manuscript's
literal signs, witnesses, interval lines and polynomial sums. It uses
